\n विक्रय मूल्य = अंकित मूल्य - छूट

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना क्रय मूल्य = 100 इकाई

\n\n

⇒ अंकित मूल्य = 100 + 100 का 42% = 100 + 42 = 142 इकाई

\n\n

⇒ दी गयी छूट = 142 का 15% = 0.15 × 142 = 21.3 इकाई

\n\n

⇒ विक्रय मूल्य = अंकित मूल्य - छूट = 142 - 21.3 = 120.7 इकाई

\n\n

⇒ लाभ = विक्रय मूल्य - क्रय मूल्य = 120.7 - 100 = 20.7 इकाई

\n\n

⇒ लाभ  \\(= \\frac{20.7}{100} \\times 100 = 20.7%\\)

\n\n

∴ लाभ का प्रतिशत, निकटतम पूर्णांक में पूर्णांकित करने पर, 21% है।

\n\n

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

కొనుగోలు ధర = 100 యూనిట్లు అనుకుందాం.

\n\n

⇒ గుర్తించబడిన ధర = 100 + 42% = 100 + 42 = 142 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

⇒ అందించబడిన రాయితీ = 142లో 15% = 0.15 × 142 = 21.3 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

⇒ అమ్మకపు ధర = గుర్తించబడిన ధర - రాయితీ = 142 - 21.3 = 120.7 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

⇒ లాభం = అమ్మకపు ధర - కొనుగోలు  ధర = 120.7 - 100 = 20.7 యూనిట్లు

\n\n

⇒ లాభ% = 20.7 100 × 100 20.7100 × 100 20.7 100 × 100 = 20.7%

\n\n

∴ లాభ శాతం, సమీప పూర్ణాంకానికి పూరించబడినది, 21%.

\n

2 = (2 ÷ 11) × h

\n

h = 11

\n

Difference between Mode and Lower limit = (f1 − f0) ÷ (2f1 − f0 − f2) × h

\n

48.5 − 46.5 = (34 − 32) ÷ (2 × 34 − 32 − 25) × h

\n

2 = (2 ÷ 11) × h

\n

h = 11

\n

∴ The correct answer is 11.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Mode (Mo) = 48.5

\n

Lower boundary of modal class (l) = 46.5

\n

Frequency of modal class (f1) = 34

\n

Frequency of preceding class (f0) = 32

\n

Frequency of succeeding class (f2) = 25

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Mode = l + [ (f1 − f0) ÷ (2f1 − f0 − f2) ] × h

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ 48.5 = 46.5 + [ (34 − 32) ÷ (2 × 34 − 32 − 25) ] × h

\n

⇒ 48.5 − 46.5 = [ 2 ÷ (68 − 57) ] × h

\n

⇒ 2 = (2 ÷ 11) × h

\n

⇒ 2 × 11 = 2h

\n

⇒ 22 = 2h

\n

⇒ h = 22 ÷ 2

\n

⇒ h = 11

\n

∴ The correct answer is 11.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Median for Grouped Data

\n

Median = l + [ (N/2 − cf) ÷ f ] × h, where l is lower limit of median class and cf is cumulative frequency of preceding class.

\n

Empirical Relationship

\n

Mode = 3 × Median − 2 × Mean. This formula is used for moderately skewed distributions.

\n

Mean for Grouped Data (Direct Method)

\n

Mean = Σfixi ÷ Σfi, where xi is the class mark (mid-value) of each interval.दिया गया है:

\n\n

बहुलक वर्ग के बाद वाले वर्ग की बारम्बारता = 25

\n\n40:Tcf7,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Side AB = BC (triangle) and BE = BC (square) ⇒ AB = BE.

\n

∠ABE = ∠ABC + ∠CBE = 60° + 90° = 150°.

\n

In isosceles ΔABE, ∠EAB = ∠AEB = (180° − 150°) ÷ 2 = 15°.

\n

Value of ∠EAB + 3∠AEB = 15° + 3(15°) = 15° + 45° = 60°.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 60°.

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Side AB = BC (triangle) and BE = BC (square) ⇒ AB = BE.

\n

∠ABE = ∠ABC + ∠CBE = 60° + 90° = 150°.

\n

In isosceles ΔABE, ∠EAB = ∠AEB = (180° − 150°) ÷ 2 = 15°.

\n

Value of ∠EAB + 3∠AEB = 15° + 3(15°) = 15° + 45° = 60°.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 60°.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: ABC is an equilateral triangle and BCDE is a square.

\n

Formula: Sum of angles in a triangle = 180°; Angles in square = 90°; Angles in equilateral triangle = 60°.

\n\n

⇒ In equilateral ΔABC, AB = BC = AC and ∠ABC = 60°.

\n

⇒ In square BCDE, BC = CD = DE = BE and ∠EBC = 90°.

\n

⇒ Total ∠ABE = ∠ABC + ∠EBC = 60° + 90° = 150°.

\n

⇒ Since AB = BC and BE = BC, we have AB = BE.

\n

⇒ In ΔABE, ∠EAB = ∠AEB (angles opposite to equal sides).

\n

⇒ ∠ABE + ∠EAB + ∠AEB = 180°

\n

⇒ 150° + 2 × ∠AEB = 180°

\n

⇒ 2 × ∠AEB = 30° ⇒ ∠AEB = 15°

\n

⇒ Similarly, ∠EAB = 15°.

\n

⇒ Required value = ∠EAB + 3∠AEB = 15° + 3(15°) = 60°.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 60°.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Equilateral Triangle Properties

\n

All three sides are equal in length, and each internal angle measures exactly 60°.

\n

Square Properties

\n

A square is a regular quadrilateral with four equal sides and four equal angles of 90°.

\n

Isosceles Triangle Theorem

\n

If two sides of a triangle are equal, then the angles opposite those sides are also equal.

\n

Angle Sum Property

\n

The sum of all interior angles of any triangle is always 180°.दिया गया है:

\n\n

एक समबाहु त्रिभुज ABC, एक वर्ग BCDE पर अधिरोपित है। 

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

समबाहु त्रिभुज में प्रत्येक कोण 60° का होता है।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना ∠EAB = y और ∠AEB = x
\n
\n

\n\n

चूँकि, ABC एक समबाहु त्रिभुज है, ∠BAC = 60°

\n\n

इसलिए, ∠ABE = 90 + 60 = 150°

\n\n

त्रिभुज EAB में,

\n\n

∠ABE + ∠EAB + ∠AEB = 180° (एक त्रिभुज में कोणों का योग 180° होता है)

\n\n

इसलिए,

\n\n

⇒ 150° + x + y = 180° 

\n\n

⇒  x + y = 30° 

\n\n

और, त्रिभुज EAB में, BE = AB

\n\n

इसलिए,∠EAB = ∠AEB

\n\n

इसलिए, x = y

\n\n

⇒ x = y = 30/2 = 15° 

\n\n

और ∠EAB + 3∠AEB = 15 + 3(15)

\n\n

सही विकल्प 60° है।

Identify frequencies: 13 appears 2 times, 15 appears 2 times.

\n

All other numbers (10, 11, 12, 14, 16, 17, 18, 20, 21, 22, 24) appear only once.

\n

The highest frequency is 2, so the modes are 13 and 15.

\n

Product of modes = 13 × 15 = 195.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 195.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: Data set = {13, 11, 16, 15, 18, 12, 13, 14, 10, 22, 15, 21, 20, 17, 24}

\n

Formula: Mode is the observation(s) with the highest frequency in the data set.

\n\n

⇒ Arranging the data in ascending order: 10, 11, 12, 13, 13, 14, 15, 15, 16, 17, 18, 20, 21, 22, 24.

\n

⇒ Frequency count:

\n

⇒ 10, 11, 12, 14, 16, 17, 18, 20, 21, 22, 24 → 1 time each.

\n

⇒ 13 → 2 times.

\n

⇒ 15 → 2 times.

\n

⇒ Since 13 and 15 have the highest frequency (2), there are two modes.

\n

⇒ Modes = 13 and 15.

\n

⇒ Product of modes = 13 × 15 = 195.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 195.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Mode of a Dataset

\n

The mode is the value that occurs most frequently in a data set. A set can have one mode (unimodal), two modes (bimodal), or more (multimodal).

\n

Empirical Relationship

\n

For a moderately skewed distribution: Mode = 3 × Median − 2 × Mean.

\n

Mean and Median

\n

Mean is the average (sum ÷ count). Median is the middle value when data is arranged in ascending or descending order.दिया गया है:

Ratio of investments A : B = 1,50,000 : 2,50,000 = 3 : 5.

\n

Divide profit ₹6,00,000 in 3 : 5 → A = ₹2,25,000 and B = ₹3,75,000.

\n

New investment ratio = (2,25,000 + 50,000) : (3,75,000 + 50,000) = 2,75,000 : 4,25,000 = 11 : 17.

\n

A’s share in new profit = (11 ÷ 28) × 4,20,000 = 11 × 15,000 = ₹1,65,000.

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹1,65,000.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Initial investment A = ₹1,50,000 | Initial investment B = ₹2,50,000

\n

Profit Project 1 = ₹6,00,000 | Profit Project 2 = ₹4,20,000

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Profit Ratio = (Capital1 × Time1) : (Capital2 × Time2)

\n\n\n\n

⇒ Investment Ratio A : B = 1,50,000 : 2,50,000 = 3 : 5

\n

⇒ Sum of ratio parts = 3 + 5 = 8 units

\n

⇒ 8 units = ₹6,00,000

\n

⇒ 1 unit = 6,00,000 ÷ 8 = ₹75,000

\n

⇒ A’s profit share (Project 1) = 3 × 75,000 = ₹2,25,000

\n

⇒ B’s profit share (Project 1) = 5 × 75,000 = ₹3,75,000

\n

⇒ A’s investment (Project 2) = 2,25,000 + 50,000 = ₹2,75,000

\n

⇒ B’s investment (Project 2) = 3,75,000 + 50,000 = ₹4,25,000

\n

⇒ New Ratio A : B = 2,75,000 : 4,25,000 = 11 : 17

\n

⇒ Total ratio units (Project 2) = 11 + 17 = 28 units

\n

⇒ A’s share in Project 2 profit = (11 ÷ 28) × 4,20,000

\n

⇒ A’s share = 11 × 15,000 = ₹1,65,000

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹1,65,000.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Partnership Profit Sharing Rule

\n

Profit is always distributed in the ratio of the product of Investment (Capital) and Time: Profit Ratio = (C1 × T1) : (C2 × T2).

\n

Types of Partnership

\n

Simple Partnership occurs when all partners invest for the same time period; Compound Partnership occurs when partners invest for different time durations.

\n

Working vs Sleeping Partner

\n

A working partner manages the business and may receive a salary or extra commission from the profit before the remaining profit is shared according to investment ratios.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

A का निवेश = 1,50,000 रुपये

\n\n

B का निवेश = 2,50,000 रुपये

\n\n

अर्जित लाभ = 6,00,000 रुपये

\n\n

दोनों एक अलग परियोजना के लिए अपने लाभ में 50,000 रुपये मिलाते हैं।

\n\n

दूसरे परियोजना से प्राप्त आय = 4,20,000 रुपये

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

\\(\\rm Profit\\ Ratio = \\frac{Investment\\ of\\ A}{Investment\\ of\\ B}\\)

\n\n

\\(\\rm Individual\\ Profit = Total\\ Profit \\times \\frac{Individual\\ Share}{Total\\ Shares}\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

A और B का निवेश अनुपात= 150000 : 250000

\n\n

⇒ अनुपात को सरल बनाने पर  = 3 : 5

\n\n

कुल लाभ = 6,00,000 रुपये

\n\n

⇒ A का लाभ का हिस्सा \\(=6,00,000 \\times \\frac{3}{8}\\)

\n\n

⇒ B का लाभ का हिस्सा = 2,25,000 रुपये

\n\n

दोनों अपने लाभ में 50,000 रुपये मिलाते हैं।

\n\n

⇒ मिलाने के बाद, A का कुल योग = 2,25,000 + 50,000

\n\n

⇒ A का कुल योग = 2,75,000 रुपये 

\n\n

नया निवेश अनुपात = 275000 : 425000 (चूँकि, B का नया योग = 2,75,000 + 50,000 = 3,25,000 + 1,00,000 (अनुपात के अनुसार, मूल लाभ का हिस्सा ) = 4,25,000)

\n\n

⇒ अनुपात को सरल बनाने पर  = 11 : 17

\n\n

दूसरे परियोजना से प्राप्त आय  = 4,20,000 रुपये

\n\n

⇒ नए लाभ में A का हिस्सा \\(= 4,20,000 \\times \\frac{11}{28}\\)

\n\n

⇒ नए लाभ में A का हिस्सा = 1,65,000 रुपये

\n\n

∴ लाभ में A का हिस्सा 1,65,000 रुपये है।

లెక్క:

\n\n

A మరియు B పెట్టుబడి నిష్పత్తి = 150000 : 250000

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ నిష్పత్తిని సూక్ష్మీకరించండి = 11 : 17

\\(\\rm Profit\\ Ratio = \\frac{Investment\\ of\\ A}{Investment\\ of\\ B}\\)

গণনা:

\n\n

A এবং B-এর বিনিয়োগের অনুপাত = 150000 : 250000

\n\n

⇒ অনুপাত সরলীকরণ করুন = 3 : 5

\n\n

মোট লাভ = 6,00,000 টাকা।

\n\n

⇒ লাভের মধ্যে A-এর অংশ \\(=6,00,000 \\times \\frac{3}{8}\\)

\n\n

⇒ লাভের মধ্যে A-এর অংশ = 2,25,000 টাকা।

\n\n

উভয়েই তাদের লাভের সাথে 50,000 টাকা যোগ করেছেন,

\n\n

⇒ যোগ করার পরে A-এর মোট = 2,25,000 + 50,000

\n\n

⇒ A-এর মোট = 2,75,000 টাকা।

\n\n

নতুন বিনিয়োগের অনুপাত = 275000 : 425000 (যেহেতু B-এর নতুন মোট = 2,75,000 + 50,000 = 3,25,000 + 1,00,000 (অনুপাত অনুযায়ী মূল লাভের অংশ) = 4,25,000)

\n\n

⇒ অনুপাত সরলীকরণ করুন = 11 : 17

\n\n

ভিন্ন প্রকল্প থেকে প্রাপ্ত ফলন = 4,20,000 টাকা।

\n\n

⇒ নতুন লাভে A-এর অংশ \\(= 4,20,000 \\times \\frac{11}{28}\\)

\n\n

⇒ নতুন লাভে A-এর অংশ = 1,65,000 টাকা।

\n\n

∴ লাভে A-এর অংশ হল 1,65,000 টাকা।

\n\n48:Tcd8,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of dimensions L : W : H = 6 : 3 : 2.

\n

TSA ratio units = 2 × (6×3 + 3×2 + 2×6) = 2 × (18 + 6 + 12) = 72 units2.

\n

72 units2 = 1800 cm2 ⇒ 1 unit2 = 25 ⇒ 1 unit = 5 cm.

\n

Volume = (6 × 3 × 2) × (5)3 = 36 × 125 = 4500 cm3.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4500 cm3.

"correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of dimensions L : W : H = 6 : 3 : 2.

\n

TSA ratio units = 2 × (6×3 + 3×2 + 2×6) = 2 × (18 + 6 + 12) = 72 units2.

\n

72 units2 = 1800 cm2 ⇒ 1 unit2 = 25 ⇒ 1 unit = 5 cm.

\n

Volume = (6 × 3 × 2) × (5)3 = 36 × 125 = 4500 cm3.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4500 cm3.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Ratio of length (l), width (w), and height (h) = 6 : 3 : 2

\n

Total Surface Area (TSA) = 1800 cm2

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Surface Area of Cuboid = 2(lw + wh + hl)

\n

Volume of Cuboid = l × w × h

\n\n

Let the length, width, and height be 6x, 3x, and 2x respectively.

\n

⇒ TSA = 2[(6x × 3x) + (3x × 2x) + (2x × 6x)]

\n

⇒ 1800 = 2[18x2 + 6x2 + 12x2]

\n

⇒ 900 = 36x2

\n

⇒ x2 = 900 ÷ 36

\n

⇒ x2 = 25

\n

⇒ x = √25 = 5

\n

Now, dimensions of the cuboid are:

\n

⇒ Length (l) = 6 × 5 = 30 cm

\n

⇒ Width (w) = 3 × 5 = 15 cm

\n

⇒ Height (h) = 2 × 5 = 10 cm

\n

Volume of the cuboid:

\n

⇒ V = l × w × h

\n

⇒ V = 30 × 15 × 10

\n

⇒ V = 4500 cm3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4500 cm3.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Lateral Surface Area of Cuboid

\n

It is the area of the four side walls of the cuboid, calculated as LSA = 2h(l + w).

\n

Diagonal of a Cuboid

\n

The length of the longest rod that can be placed inside a cuboid is given by d = √(l2 + w2 + h2).

\n

Cube Properties

\n

For a cube of side 'a', Volume = a3 and Total Surface Area = 6a2.दिया गया है:

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ ಪರಿಮಾಣ = l × w × h = (6x) × (3x) × (2x)

\n\n

⇒ ಸಂಪುಟ = 6 × 3 × 2 × x 3

\n\n

⇒ ಸಂಪುಟ = 36 × 5 3

\n\n

⇒ ಸಂಪುಟ = 36 × 125

\n\n

⇒ 4500 ಸೆಂ.ಮೀ 3

\n\n

∴ ಘನಾಕೃತಿಯ ಪರಿಮಾಣ 4500 ಸೆಂ.ಮೀ 3 ಆಗಿದೆ.

In dishonest dealer problems where buying and selling rates are same:

\n

Profit % = [(Wholesaler Weight − B's Weight) ÷ B's Weight] × 100

\n

Profit % = [(950 − 750) ÷ 750] × 100

\n

Profit % = [200 ÷ 750] × 100 = (4 ÷ 15) × 100 = 26.666...

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26.67%.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Rate of purchase = ₹180/kg | Weight received from wholesaler = 950 g for every 1000 g

\n

Rate of sale = ₹180/kg | Weight given to customer = 750 g for every 1000 g

\n

Total apples purchased = 15 kg

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Actual quantity received by B = 15 kg × (950/1000) = 14.25 kg

\n

⇒ Total Cost Price (CP) for B = 15 × ₹180 = ₹2700 (since B pays for 15kg)

\n

⇒ B sells 750 g as 1 kg. So, 14.25 kg is sold as = 14.25 ÷ 0.750 = 19 units of 1 kg

\n

⇒ Total Selling Price (SP) for B = 19 × ₹180 = ₹3420

\n

⇒ Profit = SP − CP = 3420 − 2700 = ₹720

\n

⇒ Profit % = (Profit ÷ CP) × 100

\n

⇒ Profit % = (720 ÷ 2700) × 100 = 72000 ÷ 2700 = 720 ÷ 27 = 80 ÷ 3

\n

⇒ Profit % = 26.666...

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26.67%.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Dishonest Dealer Concept

\n

When a dealer cheats while buying and selling, the overall profit multiplier is (Buying Factor × Selling Factor). Profit % = [(&text{True Weight} + &text{Gain while buying}) ÷ (&text{True Weight} − &text{Loss while selling}) − 1] × 100.

\n

Ratio Method for Profit

\n

For dishonest weights, CP : SP = Weight Given : Weight Charged. If the price per kg also changes, multiply the ratios accordingly.

\n

Effective Profit %

\n

If a person uses x% less weight, the profit % is [x ÷ (100 − x)] × 100. In this case, B uses 25% less weight (250g/1000g) on his end, but he also received 5% less weight from the wholesaler.

\n50:T8a7,<", "hn": "

दिया गया है:

\n\n

B द्वारा खरीदे गए सेब = 15 किलोग्राम, 180 रुपये/किलोग्राम की दर से

\n\n

थोक विक्रेता द्वारा उपयोग किये गये 1 किलोग्राम का वास्तविक वजन = 950 ग्राम

\n\n

B द्वारा उपयोग किये गये 1 किलोग्राम का वास्तविक वजन = 750 ग्राम

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

लाभ प्रतिशत = ((कुल विक्रय मूल्य - कुल क्रय मूल्य) / कुल क्रय मूल्य) × 100

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

खरीदे गए सेबों का कुल वजन = 15 किलोग्राम × 950 ग्राम = 14250 ग्राम

\n\n

बेचे गए सेबों का कुल वजन = 14250 ग्राम / 750 ग्राम/किलोग्राम = 19 किलोग्राम 

\n\n

कुल क्रय मूल्य = 15 किलोग्राम × 180 रुपये/किग्रा = 2700 रुपये

\n\n

कुल विक्रय मूल्य = 19 किलोग्राम × 180 रुपये/किलो = 3420 रुपये

\n\n

लाभ = कुल विक्रय मूल्य - कुल क्रय मूल्य = 3420 रुपये - 2700 रुपये = 720 रुपये

\n\n

लाभ प्रतिशत = (720/2700) × 100 ≈ 26.67%

\n\n

लेनदेन में B द्वारा अर्जित प्रतिशत लाभ 26.67% है।

\n51:T78f,

ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

B కొనుగోలు చేసిన ఆపిల్స్ = 15 కిలో, రూ. 180/కిలో చొప్పున.

\n\n

టోకు వ్యాపారి ఉపయోగించిన 1 కిలో వాస్తవ బరువు = 950 గ్రా.

\n\n

B ఉపయోగించిన 1 కిలో వాస్తవ బరువు = 750 గ్రా.

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

లాభ శాతం = ((మొత్తం అమ్మకపు ధర - మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర) / మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర) x 100

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

కొనుగోలు చేసిన ఆపిల్స్ మొత్తం బరువు = 15 kg x 950 గ్రా = 14250 గ్రా.

\n\n

అమ్మిన ఆపిల్స్ మొత్తం బరువు = 14250 గ్రా / 750 గ్రా/కిలో= 19 కిలో.

\n\n

మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర = 15 కిలో x రూ. 180/కిలో = రూ. 2700.

\n\n

మొత్తం అమ్మకపు ధర = 19 కిలో x రూ. 180/కిలో = రూ. 3420.ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

B కొనుగోలు చేసిన ఆపిల్స్ = 15 కిలో, రూ. 180/కిలో చొప్పున.

\n\n

టోకు వ్యాపారి ఉపయోగించిన 1 కిలో వాస్తవ బరువు = 950 గ్రా.

\n\n

B ఉపయోగించిన 1 కిలో వాస్తవ బరువు = 750 గ్రా.

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

లాభ శాతం = ((మొత్తం అమ్మకపు ధర - మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర) / మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర) x 100

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

కొనుగోలు చేసిన ఆపిల్స్ మొత్తం బరువు = 15 kg x 950 గ్రా = 14250 గ్రా.

\n\n

అమ్మిన ఆపిల్స్ మొత్తం బరువు = 14250 గ్రా / 750 గ్రా/కిలో= 19 కిలో.

\n\n

మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర = 15 కిలో x రూ. 180/కిలో = రూ. 2700.

\n\n

మొత్తం అమ్మకపు ధర = 19 కిలో x రూ. 180/కిలో = రూ. 3420.

\n\n

లాభం = మొత్తం అమ్మకపు ధర - మొత్తం కొనుగోలు ధర = రూ. 3420 - రూ. 2700 = రూ. 720.

\n\n

లాభ శాతం = (720 / 2700) x 100 ≈ 26.67%.

\n\n

ఈ లావాదేవీలో B స\nంపాదించిన లాభ శాతం 26.67%.

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

B দ্বারা কেনা আপেল = 15 কেজি @ 180 টাকা/কেজি।

\n\n

পাইকারি বিক্রেতা দ্বারা ব্যবহৃত 1 কেজির প্রকৃত ওজন = 950 গ্রাম।

\n\n

B দ্বারা ব্যবহৃত 1 কেজির প্রকৃত ওজন = 750 গ্রাম।

\n\n

ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

লাভের শতাংশ = ((মোট বিক্রয় মূল্য - মোট ক্রয় মূল্য) / মোট ক্রয় মূল্য) × 100

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

কেনা আপেলের মোট ওজন = 15 কেজি × 950 গ্রাম = 14250 গ্রাম।

\n\n

বিক্রি করা আপেলের মোট ওজন = 14250 গ্রাম / 750 গ্রাম/কেজি = 19 কেজি।

\n\n

মোট ক্রয় মূল্য = 15 কেজি × 180 টাকা/কেজি = 2700 টাকা।

\n\n

মোট বিক্রয় মূল্য = 19 কেজি दिलेले:

\n\n

B ने खरेदी केलेले सफरचंद = 15 किलो, 180 रुपये/किलो दराने.

\n\n

घाऊक विक्रेत्याने वापरलेले 1 किलोचे प्रत्यक्ष वजन = 950 ग्रॅम.

\n\n

B ने वापरलेले 1 किलोचे प्रत्यक्ष वजन = 750 ग्रॅम.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

नफा टक्केवारी = ((एकूण विक्री किंमत - एकूण खरेदी किंमत) / एकूण खरेदी किंमत) x 100

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

खरेदी केलेल्या सफरचंदांचे एकूण वजन = 15 किलो x 950 ग्रॅम = 14250 ग्रॅम.

\n\n

विक्री केलेल्या सफरचंदांचे एकूण वजन = 14250 ग्रॅम / 750 ग्रॅम/किलो = 19 किलो.

\n\n

एकूण खरेदी किंमत = 15 किलो x 180 रुपये/किलो = 2700 रुपये.

\n\n

एकूण विक्री किंमत = 19 किलो x 180 रुपये/किलो = 3420 रुपये.

\n\n

नफा = एकूण विक्री किंमत - एकूण खरेदी किंमत = 3420 रुपये - 2700 रुपये = 720 रुपये.

\n\n

नफा टक्केवारी = (720 / 2700) x 100 ≈ 26.67%.

\n\n

B ला या व्यवहारात झालेला नफा 26.67% आहे.

\\frac{7}{57}\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(\\frac{29}{25}\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\( \\frac{47}{7}\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Find P(A ∩ B) using De Morgan Law: P(A ∩ B) = 1 − P(Ā ∪ B̄) = 1 − 13/27 = 14/27.

\n

Calculate P(B) using Addition Rule: P(B) = P(A ∪ B) + P(A ∩ B) − P(A) = 5/9 + 14/27 − 11/18.

\n

Using LCM 54: P(B) = (30 + 28 − 33) / 54 = 25/54.

\n

Odds against B = P(B̄) : P(B) = (54 − 25) : 25 = 29 : 25.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 29/25.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

P(A ∪ B) = 5/9

\n

P(Ā ∪ B̄) = 13/27

\n

P(A) = 11/18

\n

Formula Used:

\n

1. De Morgan's Law: P(Ā ∪ B̄) = P((A ∩ B)̄) = 1 − P(A ∩ B)

\n

2. Addition Theorem: P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B) − P(A ∩ B)

\n

3. Odds against event B = P(B̄) / P(B) = (1 − P(B)) / P(B)

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ P(A ∩ B) = 1 − P(Ā ∪ B̄)

\n

⇒ P(A ∩ B) = 1 − 13/27 = 14/27

\n

⇒ P(B) = P(A ∪ B) − P(A) + P(A ∩ B)

\n

⇒ P(B) = 5/9 − 11/18 + 14/27

\n

⇒ LCM of 9, 18, 27 is 54.

\n

⇒ P(B) = (30 − 33 + 28) / 54 = 25/54

\n

⇒ P(B̄) = 1 − 25/54 = 29/54

\n

⇒ Odds against B = P(B̄) / P(B) = (29/54) / (25/54)

\n

⇒ Odds against B = 29/25

\n

∴ The correct answer is 29/25.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

De Morgan's Laws

\n

For any two events A and B, P(Ā ∩ B̄) = P((A ∪ B)̄) and P(Ā ∪ B̄) = P((A ∩ B)̄).

\n

Odds in Favor vs Odds Against

\n

Odds in favor of an event E are P(E) : P(Ē). Odds against an event E are P(Ē) : P(E).

\n

Probability Addition Theorem

\n

For any events A and B, P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B) − P(A ∩ B). If they are mutually exclusive, P(A ∩ B) = 0.दिया गया है:

\n\n

P(A ∪ B) = 5/9

\n\n

P(Ā ∪ B̄) = 13/27

\n\n

P(A) = 11/18

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

P(Ā ∪ B̄) = 1 - P(A ∩ B)

\n\n

P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B) - P(A ∩ B)

\n\n

घटना B के प्रतिकूल संयोगानुपात = P(B̄) / P(B)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

P(Ā ∪ B̄) = 1 - P(A ∩ B)

\n\n

13/27 = 1 - P(A ∩ B)

\n\n

⇒ P(A ∩ B) = 1 - 13/27

\n\n

⇒ P(A ∩ B) = 14/27

\n\n

P(A ∪ B) = P(A) + P(B) - P(A ∩ B)

\n\n

5/9 = 11/18 + P(B) - 14/27

\n\n

⇒ 5/9 = 30/54

\n\n

⇒ 11/18 = 33/54

\n\n

⇒ 14/27 = 28/54

\n\n

30/54 = 33/54 + P(B) - 28/54

\n\n

⇒ P(B) = 30/54 - 33/54 + 28/54

\n\n

⇒ P(B) = 25/54

\n\n

P(B̄) = 1 - P(B)

\n\n

⇒ P(B̄) = 1 - 25/54

\n\n

⇒ P(B̄) = 29/54

\n\n

घटना B के प्रतिकूल संयोगानुपात = P(B̄) / P(B)

\n\n

⇒ घटना B के प्रतिकूल संयोगानुपात = 29/54 / 25/54

\n\n

⇒ घटना B के प्रतिकूल संयोगानुपात = 29/25

\n\n

घटना B के प्रतिकूल संयोगानुपात 29/25 हैं।

The extra weight of the 14th person (39 kg) is distributed among the first 13 persons to equalize their average to the overall average.

\n

Total Average = Average of 13 persons + (Extra weight ÷ 13)

\n

Total Average = 78 + (39 ÷ 13) = 78 + 3 = 81 kg

\n

Weight of 14th person = Total Average + Extra = 81 + 39 = 120 kg

\n

∴ The correct answer is 120 kg.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Average weight of 13 persons = 78 kg

\n

Weight of 14th person = Average of 14 persons + 39 kg

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Average = Sum of all observations ÷ Number of observations

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

Let the average weight of all 14 persons be x kg.

\n

⇒ Total weight of 14 persons = 14 × x = 14x

\n

⇒ Weight of 14th person = x + 39

\n

⇒ Sum of weights of 13 persons = 13 × 78 = 1014 kg

\n

⇒ Total Weight = Sum of 13 persons + Weight of 14th person

\n

⇒ 14x = 1014 + (x + 39)

\n

⇒ 14x − x = 1053

\n

⇒ 13x = 1053

\n

⇒ x = 1053 ÷ 13

\n

⇒ x = 81 kg

\n

⇒ Weight of 14th person = 81 + 39 = 120 kg

\n

∴ The correct answer is 120 kg.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Average Concept

\n

Average is the central value of a set of data. Formula: Average = Total Sum ÷ Total Number.

\n

Deviation Method

\n

If a new member joins and the average increases, the new member's value = Old Average + (Increase in Average × New Strength).

\n

Sum of Observations

\n

Sum = Average × Number of observations. Any change in sum directly impacts the average by Change ÷ n.दिया गया है:

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

First discount (d1) = 20%

\n

\n

Third discount (d3) = 10%

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Successive discount of x% and y% = [x + y − (x × y)/100]%

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

\n

⇒ D12 = 20 + 35 − (20 × 35)/100

\n

⇒ D12 = 55 − 7 = 48%

\n

⇒ Now, equivalent discount for 48% and 10%:

\n

⇒ Net Discount = 48 + 10 − (48 × 10)/100

\n

⇒ Net Discount = 58 − 4.8

\n

⇒ Net Discount = 53.2%

\n

∴ The correct answer is 53.2%.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Successive Discount Formula

\n

For two successive discounts a% and b%, the single equivalent discount is (a + b − ab/100)%.

\n

Relation between MP and SP

\n

Selling Price (SP) = Marked Price (MP) × (100 − d1)/100 × (100 − d2)/100 × ...

"end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Probability of independent events with replacement = P(A) × P(B) × P(C)

\n

P(Jack) = 4/52 = 1/13

\n

P(Black Card) = 26/52 = 1/2

\n

P(Even Numbered Card) = 20/52 = 5/13 (Even cards are 2, 4, 6, 8, 10 per suit)

\n

Total Probability = (1/13) × (1/2) × (5/13) = 5/338

\n

∴ The correct answer is 5/338.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total cards in a pack = 52

\n

Condition: With replacement (Events are independent)

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Probability P(E) = Number of favorable outcomes ÷ Total number of outcomes

\n

For independent events, P(A and B and C) = P(A) × P(B) × P(C)

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Total cards in a pack = 52

\n

⇒ Number of Jacks = 4 (Jack of Spades, Clubs, Hearts, Diamonds)

\n

⇒ P(First card is a Jack) = 4/52 = 1/13

\n

⇒ Number of black cards = 26 (13 Spades + 13 Clubs)

\n

⇒ P(Second card is black) = 26/52 = 1/2

\n

⇒ Numbered cards are 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10. Even numbered cards = {2, 4, 6, 8, 10}

\n

⇒ Total even numbered cards = 5 per suit × 4 suits = 20

\n

⇒ P(Third card is an even numbered card) = 20/52 = 5/13

\n

⇒ Combined Probability = (1/13) × (1/2) × (5/13)

\n

⇒ Combined Probability = 5 / (13 × 2 × 13) = 5 / (26 × 13)

\n

⇒ Combined Probability = 5/338

\n

∴ The correct answer is 5/338.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Independent Events with Replacement

\n

When objects are replaced, the total number of outcomes remains the same for every draw, making the events independent.

\n

Dependent Events without Replacement

\n

If cards were drawn without replacement, the denominator would decrease: P(A) × P(B|A) × P(C|A,B) with totals 52, 51, and 50 respectively.

\n

Face Cards and Numbered Cards

\n

A standard deck has 12 face cards (J, Q, K) and 36 numbered cards (2 to 10). Ace is usually not counted as a numbered card in competitive exams unless specified.दिया गया है:

To find the number of 4-legged animals (Monkeys) in a group of 2-legged and 4-legged animals:

\n

Number of Monkeys = (Total Legs − 2 × Total Heads) ÷ 2

\n

⇒ Number of Monkeys = (148 − 2 × 54) ÷ 2

\n

⇒ Number of Monkeys = (148 − 108) ÷ 2 = 40 ÷ 2 = 20

\n

∴ The correct answer is 20 monkeys.

\n\n

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">20</font></font>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">16</font></font>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">18</font></font>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">14</font></font>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

To find the number of 4-legged animals (Monkeys) in a group of 2-legged and 4-legged animals:

\n

Number of Monkeys = (Total Legs − 2 × Total Heads) ÷ 2

\n

⇒ Number of Monkeys = (148 − 2 × 54) ÷ 2

\n

⇒ Number of Monkeys = (148 − 108) ÷ 2 = 40 ÷ 2 = 20

\n

∴ The correct answer is 20 monkeys.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total number of heads = 54

\n

Total number of legs = 148

\n

Number of legs for a human = 2

\n

Number of legs for a monkey = 4

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the number of monkeys be M and humans be H.

\n

⇒ Total heads: M + H = 54 → (Eq. 1)

\n

⇒ Total legs: 4M + 2H = 148 → (Eq. 2)

\n

⇒ Multiply (Eq. 1) by 2: 2M + 2H = 108 → (Eq. 3)

\n

⇒ Subtract (Eq. 3) from (Eq. 2): (4M − 2M) + (2H − 2H) = 148 − 108

\n

⇒ 2M = 40

\n

⇒ M = 40 ÷ 2 = 20

\n

∴ The correct answer is 20 monkeys.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Alligation Method for Heads/Legs

\n

Ratio of 4-legged to 2-legged animals = (Average legs per head − 2) : (4 − Average legs per head).

\n

Linear Equation Method

\n

Solving systems of equations x + y = Total Heads and Ax + By = Total Legs, where A and B are the number of legs per animal.

\n

Assumption Shortcut

\n

Assume all animals have 2 legs: Total legs would be 2 × Heads. Any excess legs (Total Legs − Calculated Legs) belong to the 4-legged animals in pairs.दिया गया है:

विपिन किसी यात्रा की एक तिहाई दूरी 30 किमी/घंटा की गति से और शेष दूरी 40 किमी/घंटा की गति से तय करता है। यदि उसे यात्रा पूरी करने में कुल 12 घंटे लगे, तो यात्रा की कुल दूरी (किमी में) कितनी थी?

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">432</font></font>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">450</font></font>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">445</font></font>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<font style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font style="vertical-align: inherit;">412</font></font>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

LCM of speeds 30 and 40 = 120.

\n

Let 1/3 distance = 120 km, then 2/3 distance = 240 km.

\n

Total hypothetical distance = 120 + 240 = 360 km.

\n

Total time taken = (120 ÷ 30) + (240 ÷ 40) = 4 + 6 = 10 hours.

\n

Given time is 12 hours ⇒ Actual distance = (360 ÷ 10) × 12 = 432 km.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 432 km.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total Time = 12 hours

\n

Speed 1 (for 1/3 distance) = 30 km/h

\n

Speed 2 (for 2/3 distance) = 40 km/h

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Time = Distance ÷ Speed

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the total distance be x km.

\n

⇒ Distance 1 = x/3 km and Distance 2 = 2x/3 km.

\n

⇒ Time 1 + Time 2 = 12

\n

⇒ (x/3) ÷ 30 + (2x/3) ÷ 40 = 12

\n

⇒ x/90 + 2x/120 = 12

\n

⇒ x/90 + x/60 = 12

\n

⇒ (2x + 3x) ÷ 180 = 12

\n

⇒ 5x ÷ 180 = 12

\n

⇒ x ÷ 36 = 12

\n

⇒ x = 36 × 12 = 432 km.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 432 km.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Average Speed Formula

\n

Average speed is calculated as Total Distance ÷ Total Time. If the distances are equal, the formula is 2uv ÷ (u+v).

\n

Distance, Speed, and Time Relation

\n

Distance = Speed × Time. When distance is constant, speed is inversely proportional to time (S1 ÷ S2 = T2 ÷ T1).

\n

Conversion of Units

\n

To convert km/h to m/s, multiply by 5/18. To convert m/s to km/h, multiply by 18/5.दिया गया है:

\n\n

एक तिहाई दूरी के लिए गति = 30 किमी/घंटा

\n\n

शेष दूरी के लिए गति = 40 किमी/घंटा

\n\n

कुल समय = 12 घंटे

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

कुल दूरी = दूरी1 + दूरी2

\n\n

दूरी = गति x समय

Shortcut Trick\n

Transfer 1 (20L to B): Ratio Spirit(S) : Water(W) is 7 : 9 ⇒ S = 8.75, W = 11.25.

\n

In A: Remaining 60L (S=26.25, W=33.75) + 20L W ⇒ S=26.25, W=53.75 (Ratio 21 : 43).

\n

Transfer 2 (32L to B): Ratio S : W is 21 : 43 ⇒ S = 10.5, W = 21.5.

\n

Total in B: S = 8.75 + 10.5 = 19.25 and W = 11.25 + 21.5 = 32.75.

\n

Ratio Water : Spirit in B = 32.75 : 19.25 = 131 : 77.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 131 ∶ 77.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total Volume (A) = 80 litres | Initial Ratio (S : W) = 7 : 9

\n

Initial S = (7 ÷ 16) × 80 = 35 litres | Initial W = (9 ÷ 16) × 80 = 45 litres

\n\n\n\n

Step 1: First transfer to B

\n

⇒ Quantity transferred = 20 litres

\n

⇒ S in B1 = (7/16) × 20 = 8.75 litres

\n

⇒ W in B1 = (9/16) × 20 = 11.25 litres

\n

Step 2: Change in Container A

\n

⇒ Remaining S in A = 35 − 8.75 = 26.25 litres

\n

⇒ Remaining W in A = 45 − 11.25 = 33.75 litres

\n

⇒ After adding 20L water: Total W in A = 33.75 + 20 = 53.75 litres

\n

⇒ New ratio S : W in A = 26.25 : 53.75 = 2625 : 5375 = 21 : 43

\n

Step 3: Second transfer to B

\n

⇒ Quantity transferred = 32 litres

\n

⇒ S in B2 = (21/64) × 32 = 10.5 litres

\n

⇒ W in B2 = (43/64) × 32 = 21.5 litres

\n

Step 4: Final Ratio in B

\n

⇒ Total Spirit in B = 8.75 + 10.5 = 19.25 litres

\n

⇒ Total Water in B = 11.25 + 21.5 = 32.75 litres

\n

⇒ Water : Spirit = 32.75 : 19.25 = 3275 : 1925

\n

⇒ Dividing by 25: 131 : 77

\n

∴ The correct answer is 131 ∶ 77.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Ratio Distribution Rule

\n

When a part of a mixture is removed, the ratio of ingredients in the removed portion and the remaining portion remains the same as the original ratio.

\n

Replacement Formula

\n

Quantity of original liquid remaining after n replacements: Cn = C0(1 − x/V)n, where x is volume replaced and V is total volume.

\n

Compound Ratio of Mixtures

\n

If two mixtures with ratios a:b and c:d are mixed in ratio x:y, the final ratio of the first component is (ax/(a+b) + cy/(c+d)) / (bx/(a+b) + dy/(c+d)).

दिया गया है:

\n\n

7 ∶ 9 के अनुपात में स्पिरिट और पानी का 80 लीटर मिश्रण एक पात्र A में उस्प्थित है।

\n\n

मिश्रण का 20 लीटर दूसरे पात्र B में स्थानांतरित किया जाता है।

\n\n

पात्र A में 20 लीटर पानी भरा हुआ है।

\n\n

32 लीटर मिश्रण को फिर से पात्र B में स्थानांतरित कर दिया जाता है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

A में प्रारंभिक स्पिरिट = (A में कुल मिश्रण) x (स्प्रिट का अनुपात) / (अनुपातों का योग)

\n\n

A में प्रारंभिक पानी = (A में कुल मिश्रण) x (पानी का अनुपात) / (अनुपातों का योग)

\n\n

स्थानांतरित स्पिरिट की मात्रा = (स्थानांतरित मिश्रण) x (A में प्रारंभिक स्पिरिट) / (A में कुल मिश्रण)

\n\n

पानी की स्थानांतरित मात्रा = (स्थानांतरित मिश्रण) x (A में प्रारंभिक पानी) / (A में कुल मिश्रण)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

A में प्रारंभिक स्पिरिट = 80 x 7/16

\n\n

⇒ A में प्रारंभिक स्पिरिट = 35 लीटर

\n\n

A में प्रारंभिक जल = 80 x 9/16

\n\n

⇒ A में प्रारंभिक पानी = 45 लीटर

\n\n

B में 20 लीटर स्थानांतरित करने के बाद, स्थानांतरित स्पिरिट = 20 x 35/80

\n\n

⇒ B में स्थानांतरित स्पिरिट = 8.75 लीटर

\n\n

B में स्थानांतरित किया गया पानी = 20 x 45/80

\n\n

⇒ B में स्थानांतरित किया गया पानी = 11.25 लीटर

\n\n

A में शेष स्पिरिट = 35 - 8.75

\n\n

⇒ A में शेष स्पिरिट = 26.25 लीटर

\n\n

A में शेष पानी = 45 - 11.25 + 20

\n\n

⇒ A में शेष पानी = 53.75 लीटर

\n\n

अब, 32 लीटर मिश्रण को पात्र B में स्थानांतरित किया जाता है:

\n\n

स्थानांतरित स्पिरिट = 32 x 26.25 / 80

\n\n

⇒ पात्र B में स्थानांतरित स्पिरिट = 10.5 लीटर

\n\n

स्थानांतरित जल = 32 x 53.75 / 80

\n\n

⇒ पात्र B में स्थानांतरित किया गया पानी = 21.5 लीटर

\n\n

पात्र B में कुल स्पिरिट = 8.75 + 10.5

\n\n

⇒ पात्र B में कुल स्पिरिट = 19.25 लीटर

\n\n

पात्र B में कुल पानी = 11.25 + 21.5

\n\n

⇒ B में कुल पानी = 32.75 लीटर

\n\n

पात्र B में पानी और स्पिरिट का अनुपात = 32.75 / 19.25

\n\n

⇒ B में पानी और स्पिरिट का अनुपात = 131/77

\n\n

पात्र B में मिश्रण में पानी और स्पिरिट का अनुपात 131 ∶ 77 के बराबर है।

Edge of small cube = 3√64 = 4 cm.

Edge of large cube = 48 cm

\n

Formula Used:

⇒ Volume of the large cube = 48 × 48 × 48

\n

⇒ Volume of the large cube = 110592 cm3

⇒ Volume of one small cube = 64 cm3

\n

⇒ Number of small cubes (n) = 110592 ÷ 64

\n

⇒ n = 1728

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1728 cubes.

Additional Information

\n

Melting and Recasting Rule

\n

When a solid is melted and recast into another shape (or multiple smaller shapes) without wastage, the total volume remains constant.

\n

Properties of a Cube

\n

For a cube of side 'a', Volume = a3, Total Surface Area = 6a2, and Diagonal = a√3.

\n

Scaling Factor in Volume

\n

If the dimensions of a 3D object are scaled by a factor 'k', its volume is scaled by a factor of k3.दिया गया है:

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ बड़े घन का आयतन = 110592 cm3

\n\n

छोटे घनों की संख्या = बड़े घन का आयतन/एक छोटे घन का आयतन

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

∴ प्राप्त छोटे घनों की संख्या 1728 है।

At a point in the road between pillars, the elevations of the pillars are x° and y° so that tan x° = \\(\\frac{2}{5}\\), tan y° = \\(\\frac{3}{5}\\), then the height of each pillar is: ", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "39 m"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "36 m"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "42 m"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "33 m"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "150 मीटर चौड़ी एक सड़क के दोनों ओर समान ऊँचाई के दो खंभे खड़े हैं। खंभों के बीच सड़क के एक बिंदु पर, खंभों की ऊँचाई x° और y° इस प्रकार है कि tan x° = \\(\\frac{2}{5}\\), tan y° = \\(\\frac{3}{5}\\) है, तो प्रत्येक खंभे की ऊँचाई है:", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "39&nbsp;मीटर"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "36&nbsp;मीटर"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "42&nbsp;मीटर"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "33&nbsp;मीटर"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Height of pillars (h) = Road Width ÷ (cot x + cot y)

\n

Given tan x = 2/5 → cot x = 5/2

\n

Given tan y = 3/5 → cot y = 5/3

\n

h = 150 ÷ (5/2 + 5/3) = 150 ÷ (25/6) = 150 × 6 ÷ 25

\n

h = 6 × 6 = 36 m

\n

∴ The correct answer is 36 m.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Road width (W) = 150 m

\n

Angle of elevation 1 = x, where tan x = 2/5

\n

Angle of elevation 2 = y, where tan y = 3/5

\n\n

⇒ Let the height of each pillar be h.

\n

⇒ Let d1 be the distance from the point to the first pillar and d2 be the distance from the point to the second pillar.

\n

⇒ d1 + d2 = 150

\n

⇒ In first triangle: tan x = h/d1 ⇒ 2/5 = h/d1 ⇒ d1 = 5h/2

\n

⇒ In second triangle: tan y = h/d2 ⇒ 3/5 = h/d2 ⇒ d2 = 5h/3

\n

⇒ 5h/2 + 5h/3 = 150

\n

⇒ 5h × (1/2 + 1/3) = 150

\n

⇒ 5h × (5/6) = 150

\n

⇒ 25h/6 = 150

\n

⇒ h = (150 × 6) ÷ 25

\n

⇒ h = 6 × 6 = 36 m

\n

∴ The correct answer is 36 m.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Height and Distance Relation

\n

For a point between two objects of same height h at distance W, h = W ÷ (cot α + cot β), where α and β are angles of elevation.

\n

Angle of Elevation

\n

The angle between the horizontal line of sight and the object when the object is above the observer level.

\n

Standard Trig Ratios

\n

tan θ = Opposite / Adjacent and cot θ = Adjacent / Opposite. They are reciprocals of each other.दिया गया है:

Simultaneous Linear Equations

\n

Equations involving two variables (x, y) can be solved by making the coefficients of one variable the same and subtracting/adding the equations.

\n

Substitution Method

\n

\n

Cost Calculation Rule

\n

Given P ∶ Q = 3 ∶ 4 and Q ∶ R = 4 ∶ 7.

Since the value of Q is common (4 units) in both ratios, we can directly write P ∶ Q ∶ R = 3 ∶ 4 ∶ 7.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

\n

Formula Used:

\n

If a ∶ b and b ∶ c are given, and the value of 'b' is same in both, then a ∶ b ∶ c = a ∶ b ∶ c.

\n

Calculations:

\n

\n

\n

⇒ Ratio = 7x ∶ 11x ∶ 10x

\n

⇒ Ratio = 7 ∶ 11 ∶ 10

\n

∴ The correct answer is 7 ∶ 11 ∶ 10.

Additional Information

\n

Compounded Ratio

\n

The compounded ratio of two ratios a ∶ b and c ∶ d is found by multiplying the corresponding terms: (a × c) ∶ (b × d).

\n

Duplicate and Sub-duplicate Ratio

\n

The duplicate ratio of a ∶ b is a2 ∶ b2, and the sub-duplicate ratio is √a ∶ √b.

\n

Normalization of Ratios

\n

To combine ratios like a ∶ b and b ∶ c where 'b' values are different, multiply each ratio by a constant to make the 'b' terms equal.

Effective rate for half-yearly compounding = 20% ÷ 2 = 10%.

\n

Ratio of Amount to Principal (A/P) = 14,641 / 10,000 = (1.4641/1).

\n

Since (1 + 10/100)n = 1.1n, we observe that 1.14 = 1.4641.

\n

Total periods (n) = 4 half-years.

\n

Time in years = 4 ÷ 2 = 2 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2 years.

Alternate Method

\n

\n

\n

\n\n\n\n

⇒ n = 4 periods

\n

⇒ Since each period is 6 months, Total Time = 4 × 6 = 24 months = 2 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2 years.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

CI Compounded Half-Yearly

\n

When interest is compounded half-yearly, the annual rate is divided by 2 and the time (in years) is multiplied by 2.

\n

CI Compounded Quarterly

\n

When interest is compounded quarterly, the annual rate is divided by 4 and the time (in years) is multiplied by 4.

\n

Effective Rate Formula

\n

For two periods with rate r%, the effective rate is [r + r + (r × r)/100] %.

\n

Difference between CI and SI

\n

For 2 years, CI − SI = P × (R/100)2. For 3 years, CI − SI = P × (R/100)2 × (3 + R/100).दिया गया है:

\n\n

मूलधन (P) = 10,000 रुपये

\n\n

वार्षिक ब्याज दर (R) = 20%

\n\n

चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज (CI) = 4,641 रुपये

\n\n

चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज अर्धवार्षिक रूप से दिया जाता है

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज सूत्र: \\(( A = P \\left(1 + \\frac{r}{n}\\right)^{nt} )\\)

\n\n

जहाँ A = मिश्रधन, P = मूलधन, r = वार्षिक ब्याज दर, n = प्रति वर्ष ब्याज चक्रवृद्धि की संख्या, t = वर्षों में समय

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

चूँकि चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज अर्धवार्षिक रूप से दिया जाता है, इसलिए प्रति अवधि की दर और प्रति वर्ष अवधि की संख्या को समायोजित करने की आवश्यकता है:

\n\n

r = 20% = 0.20

\n\n

n = 2 (चूँकि चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज अर्धवार्षिक रूप से दिया जाता है)

\n\n

कुल मिश्रधन (A) = मूलधन + चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज

\n\n

⇒ A = 10,000 + 4,641

\n\n

⇒ A = 14,641

\n\n

सूत्र का प्रयोग करने पर:

\n\n

\\( A = P \\left(1 + \\frac{r}{n}\\right)^{nt} \\)

\n\n

\\( 14,641 = 10,000 \\left(1 + \\frac{0.20}{2}\\right)^{2t} \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\( 14,641 = 10,000 \\left(1 + 0.10\\right)^{2t} \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\( 14,641 = 10,000 \\left(1.10\\right)^{2t} \\)

\n\n

दोनों पक्षों को 10,000 से विभाजित करने पर:

\n\n

⇒ \\( 1.4641 = \\left(1.10\\right)^{2t} \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((1.10)^4 = \\left(1.10\\right)^{2t} \\)

\n\n

⇒ t = 2

\n\n

\n\n7c:Tb4d,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of Kamal : (Hari + Lalit) = 142 : 100 = 71 : 50.

\n

Total parts = 71 + 50 = 121 units.

\n

121 units = 6050 ⇒ 1 unit = 50.

\n

Hari + Lalit = 50 × 50 = 2500.

\n

Lalit = 8% of 6050 = 484.

\n

Hari = 2500 − 484 = 2016.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2016 votes.

\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Ratio of Kamal : (Hari + Lalit) = 142 : 100 = 71 : 50.

\n

Total parts = 71 + 50 = 121 units.

\n

121 units = 6050 ⇒ 1 unit = 50.

\n

Hari + Lalit = 50 × 50 = 2500.

\n

Lalit = 8% of 6050 = 484.

\n

Hari = 2500 − 484 = 2016.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2016 votes.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total polled votes = 6050

\n

Lalit's share = 8% of total votes

\n

Kamal's share = (Hari + Lalit) + 42% of (Hari + Lalit)

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Votes obtained by Lalit = 8% of 6050

\n

⇒ Lalit = (8 × 6050) ÷ 100 = 484

\n

⇒ Let votes obtained by (Hari + Lalit) = x

\n

⇒ Kamal's votes = x + 0.42x = 1.42x

\n

⇒ Total votes = Kamal + (Hari + Lalit)

\n

⇒ 6050 = 1.42x + x

\n

⇒ 6050 = 2.42x

\n

⇒ x = 6050 ÷ 2.42 = 2500

\n

⇒ Hari + Lalit = 2500

\n

⇒ Hari + 484 = 2500

\n

⇒ Hari = 2500 − 484 = 2016

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2016 votes.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Election Vote Concepts

\n

Total Votes Polled = Valid Votes + Invalid Votes. In this case, all polled votes are valid.

\n

Percentage Comparison

\n

If A is r% more than B, then A = B × (1 + r/100). If A is r% less than B, then A = B × (1 − r/100).

\n

Ratio Partitioning

\n

To divide a total 'T' into a ratio a : b, the first part is [a / (a+b)] × T and the second part is [b / (a+b)] × T.दिया गया है:

\n\n

कुल डाले गए मत = 6050

\n\n

कमल को हरि और ललित को मिले कुल मतों से 42% अधिक मत मिले।

\n\n

ललित को कुल मतदान का 8% मत प्राप्त हुए।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

कुल मत = हरि के मत + कमल के मत + ललित के मत

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

ललित को प्राप्त मत = 6050 का 8%

\n\n

⇒ ललित के मत = 0.08 × 6050 = 484

\n\n

माना हरि को प्राप्त मत H हैं और कमल को प्राप्त मत K हैं।

\n\n

प्रश्न के अनुसार, कमल को हरि और ललित को मिले कुल मतों से 42% अधिक मत मिले।

\n\n

⇒ K = 1.42 × (H + 484)

\n\n

साथ ही, कुल मत हरि, कमल और ललित के मतों का योग है।

\n\n

⇒ H + K + 484 = 6050

\n\n

K का मान प्रतिस्थापित करने पर:

\n\n

⇒ H + 1.42 × (H + 484) + 484 = 6050

\n\n

⇒ H + 1.42H + 687.28 + 484 = 6050

\n\n

⇒ 2.42H + 1171.28 = 6050

\n\n

⇒ 2.42H = 6050 - 1171.28

\n\n

⇒ 2.42H = 4878.72

\n\n

⇒ H = 4878.72 / 2.42

\n\n

⇒ H = 2016

\n\n

हरि को मिले मतों की संख्या 2016 है।

\n\n

Shortcut Trick 

\n\n7e:Te0a,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Total Work = LCM(12.5, 18.75, 15) = 75 units.

\n

Eff(A+B) = 6, Eff(B+C) = 4, Eff(C+A) = 5.

\n

Combined Eff(A+B+C) = (6 + 4 + 5) ÷ 2 = 7.5 units/day.

\n

Eff(C) = 7.5 − 6 = 1.5 units/day | Eff(D) = 40% of 1.5 = 0.6 units/day.

\n

Total Time = 75 ÷ (7.5 + 0.6) = 75 ÷ 8.1 = 250/27 = 9 7/27 days.

Total Work = LCM(12.5, 18.75, 15) = 75 units.

\n

Eff(A+B) = 6, Eff(B+C) = 4, Eff(C+A) = 5.

\n

Combined Eff(A+B+C) = (6 + 4 + 5) ÷ 2 = 7.5 units/day.

\n

Eff(C) = 7.5 − 6 = 1.5 units/day | Eff(D) = 40% of 1.5 = 0.6 units/day.

\n

\n

∴ The correct answer is 9 7/27 days.

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Time (A + B) = 12.5 days = 25/2 days

\n

Time (B + C) = 18.75 days = 75/4 days

\n

Time (C + A) = 15 days

\n

Efficiency of D = 40% of Efficiency of C

\n\n\n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n
PersonDaysTotal WorkEfficiency
A + B12.5756
B + C18.754
C + A155
\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Total efficiency of (A+B), (B+C), and (C+A) = 6 + 4 + 5 = 15

\n

⇒ 2 × Efficiency (A + B + C) = 15

\n

⇒ Efficiency (A + B + C) = 15 ÷ 2 = 7.5 units/day

\n

⇒ Efficiency of C = Efficiency (A + B + C) − Efficiency (A + B)

\n

⇒ Efficiency of C = 7.5 − 6 = 1.5 units/day

\n

⇒ Efficiency of D = 40/100 × 1.5 = 0.6 units/day

\n

⇒ Combined efficiency (A + B + C + D) = 7.5 + 0.6 = 8.1 units/day

\n

⇒ Total days = Total Work ÷ Combined Efficiency = 75 ÷ 8.1

\n

⇒ Total days = 750 ÷ 81 = 250 ÷ 27 = 9 7/27 days

\n

∴ The correct answer is 9 7/27 days.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Work and Efficiency Relationship

\n

Efficiency is inversely proportional to time. If a person takes 'n' days to complete a work, their efficiency is 1/n per day.

\n

Combined Work Formula

\n

For pairs (A+B, B+C, C+A), individual efficiency of A = [Eff(A+B) + Eff(C+A) − Eff(B+C)] ÷ 2.

\n

Efficiency Ratios

\n

If X is k% as efficient as Y, then Efficiency(X) = (k/100) × Efficiency(Y).दिया गया है:

\n\n

A और B मिलकर किसी कार्य को 12.5 दिनों में पूरा कर सकते हैं।

\n\n

B और C मिलकर उसी कार्य को 18.75 दिनों में पूरा कर सकते हैं।

\n\n

C और A मिलकर उसी कार्य को 15 दिनों में पूरा कर सकते हैं।

\n\n

D, C से 40% अधिक दक्ष है।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

सबसे पहले, उभयनिष्ठ कार्य आधार प्राप्त करने के लिए 12.5, 18.75 और 15 के लघुतम समापवर्त्य की गणना करते हैं।

\n\n

भिन्नों में परिवर्तित करने के बाद हर क्रमशः 8, 16 और 4 हैं:

\n\n

12.5 = 12.5/1 = 125/10 = 25/2

\n\n

18.75 = 18.75/1 = 187.5/10 = 375/20 = 75/4

\n\n

15 = 15/1 = 15

\n\n

2, 4, और 1 (हर) का लघुतम समापवर्त्य 4 है। आइए 80 के उभयनिष्ठ आधार (8, 16 और 4 का लघुतम समापवर्त्य) का उपयोग कीजिए जो गणना के लिए अधिक सरल है:

\n\n

A और B एक साथ कार्य करते हैं = 80 / 12.5 = 6.4 इकाई/दिन

\n\n

B और C एक साथ कार्य करते हैं = 80/18.75 = 4.267 इकाई/दिन

\n\n

C और A एक साथ कार्य करते हैं = 80/15 = 5.333 इकाई/दिन

\n\n

दरें जोड़ें और व्यक्तिगत दरों का हल करने पर:

\n\n

A + B = 6.4

\n\n

B + C = 4.267

\n\n

C + A = 5.333

\n\n

2A + 2B + 2C = 16 (तीनों समीकरणों को जोड़ने पर)

\n\n

A + B + C = 8 इकाई/दिन (2 से विभाजित करना)

\n\n

C की दर ज्ञात करने के लिए:

\n\n

A + C = 5.333, और A + B + C से B + C को घटाने पर:

\n\n

A + B + C - (B + C) = 8 - 4.267 = 3.733 (A की दर)

\n\n

तब C की दर है (A + C) - A = 5.333 - 3.733 = 1.6 इकाई/दिन

\n\n

D की दर है = C का 40% = 0.4 x 1.6 = 0.64 इकाई/दिन

\n\n

A, B, C, और D की कुल दर = A + B + C + D = 8 + 0.64 = 8.64 इकाई/दिन

\n\n

A, B, C और D को कार्य पूरा करने में लगा समय = 80 / 8.64 ≈ 9.26 दिन = \\(9 \\frac{7}{27} \\)

\n\n

A, B, C और D की टीम कार्य को लगभग 9.26 या \\(9 \\frac{7}{27} \\) दिनों में पूरा कर सकती है।

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n80:Tbba,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Total present age of A, B and C = 20 × 3 = 60 years.

\n

Two years ago, (A − 2) + (B − 2) = (C − 2) + 6 ⇒ (A + B) − C = 8.

\n

We have: (A + B) + C = 60 and (A + B) − C = 8.

\n

By subtracting the equations: 2C = 60 − 8 = 52 ⇒ C = 26 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26 years.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Average age of A, B, C = 20 years.

\n

Condition 2 years ago: (Sum of A & B) = (Age of C) + 6.

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Age = Average × Number of persons.

\n Shortcut Trick\n

Total present age of A, B and C = 20 × 3 = 60 years.

\n

Two years ago, (A − 2) + (B − 2) = (C − 2) + 6 ⇒ (A + B) − C = 8.

\n

We have: (A + B) + C = 60 and (A + B) − C = 8.

\n

By subtracting the equations: 2C = 60 − 8 = 52 ⇒ C = 26 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26 years.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Average age of A, B, C = 20 years.

\n

Condition 2 years ago: (Sum of A & B) = (Age of C) + 6.

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Age = Average × Number of persons.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Present sum A + B + C = 20 × 3 = 60 --- (i)

\n

⇒ Ages 2 years ago: (A−2), (B−2), (C−2)

\n

⇒ According to question: (A − 2) + (B − 2) = (C − 2) + 6

\n

⇒ A + B − 4 = C + 4

\n

⇒ A + B − C = 8 --- (ii)

\n

⇒ From (i), A + B = 60 − C

\n

⇒ Substitute in (ii): (60 − C) − C = 8

\n

⇒ 60 − 2C = 8

\n

⇒ 2C = 52

\n

⇒ C = 26 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 26 years.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Average Age Concept

\n

If the average age of n persons is X years, then after t years, the average age will be (X + t) years.

\n

Sum of Ages

\n

To find the sum of ages of a group, multiply the average age by the number of members in that group (Sum = Average × n).

\n

Age Difference

\n

The difference between the ages of two individuals remains constant regardless of the number of years passed.दिया गया है:

\n\n

A, B और C की औसत आयु = 20 वर्ष है।

\n\n

दो वर्ष पूर्व A और B की आयु का योग = C की दो वर्ष पूर्व की आयु + 6

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

\\( \\rm Average\\ Age = \\frac{Sum\\ of\\ Ages}{Number\\ of\\ Persons} \\)

\n\n

\\( \\rm Sum\\ of\\ Ages = Average\\ Age \\times Number\\ of\\ Persons \\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

A, B और C की वर्तमान आयु का योग = 20 × 3 = 60 वर्ष

\n\n

⇒ माना C की वर्तमान आयु = x वर्ष

\n\n

⇒ A और B की वर्तमान आयु का योग = (60 - x) वर्ष

\n\n

⇒ दो वर्ष पूर्व, A और B की आयु का योग = (60 - x) - 2×2 = (60 - x - 4)

\n\n

⇒ दो वर्ष पूर्व, C की आयु = x - 2

\n\n

⇒ दिए गए अनुसार, (60 - x - 4) = (x - 2) + 6

\n\n

⇒ 60 - x - 4 = x + 4

\n\n

⇒ 56 - x = x + 4

\n\n

⇒ 56 - 4 = x + x

\n\n

⇒ 52 = 2x

\n\n

⇒ x = 26

\n\n

∴ C की वर्तमान आयु 26 वर्ष है।

Given:

\n

\\( \\frac{\\sqrt{1.24} \\times \\sqrt{2.79}}{\\sqrt{2.64} \\times \\sqrt{5.94}} \\) = ?

\n

Formula used:

\n

\\( \\sqrt{a} \\times \\sqrt{b} = \\sqrt{a \\times b} \\)

\n

\\( \\frac{\\sqrt{a}}{\\sqrt{b}} = \\sqrt{\\frac{a}{b}} \\)

\n

Calculation:

\n

\\( \\frac{\\sqrt{1.24} \\times \\sqrt{2.79}}{\\sqrt{2.64} \\times \\sqrt{5.94}} \\)

\n

⇒ \\( \\sqrt{\\frac{1.24 \\times 2.79}{2.64 \\times 5.94}} \\)

\n

⇒ \\( \\sqrt{\\frac{124 \\times 279}{264 \\times 594}} \\)

\n

⇒ \\( \\sqrt{\\frac{(4 \\times 31) \\times (9 \\times 31)}{(4 \\times 66) \\times (9 \\times 66)}} \\)

\n

&r", "or": "", "as": "", "ks": "", "kok": "", "mni": "", "ne": "", "pa": "

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ:

\n\n

A, B, ਅਤੇ C ਦੀ ਔਸਤ ਉਮਰ = 20 ਸਾਲ।

\n\n

ਦੋ ਸਾਲ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ A ਅਤੇ B ਦੀਆਂ ਉਮਰਾਂ ਦਾ ਜੋੜ = ਦੋ ਸਾਲ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ C ਦੀ ਉਮਰ + 6।

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n\n

\\( \\rm ਔਸਤ\\ ਉਮਰ = \\frac{ਉਮਰਾਂ\\ ਦਾ\\ ਜੋੜ}{ਵਿਅਕਤੀਆਂ\\ ਦੀ\\ ਗਿਣਤੀ} \\)

\n\n

\\( \\rm ਉਮਰਾਂ\\ ਦਾ\\ ਜੋੜ = ਔਸਤ\\ ਉਮਰ \\times ਵਿਅਕਤੀਆਂ\\ ਦੀ\\ ਗਿਣਤੀ} \\)

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

A, B, ਅਤੇ C ਦੀ ਮੌਜੂਦਾ ਉਮਰ ਦਾ ਜੋੜ = 20 x 3 = 60 ਸਾਲ।

\n\n

⇒ ਮੰਨ ਲਓ C ਦੀ ਮੌਜੂਦਾ ਉਮਰ = x ਸਾਲ।

\n\n

⇒ A ਅਤੇ B ਦੀ ਮੌਜੂਦਾ ਉਮਰ ਦਾ ਜੋੜ = (60 - x) ਸਾਲ।

\n\n

⇒ ਦੋ ਸਾਲ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ, A ਅਤੇ B ਦੀਆਂ ਉਮਰਾਂ ਦਾ ਜੋੜ = (60 - x) - 2x2 = (60 - x - 4)।

\n\n

⇒ ਦੋ ਸਾਲ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ, C ਦੀ ਉਮਰ = x - 2।

\n\n

⇒ ਦਿੱਤੇ ਅਨੁਸਾਰ, (60 - x - 4) = (x - 2) + 6

\n\n

⇒ 60 - x - 4 = x + 4

\n\n

⇒ 56 - x = x + 4

\n\n

⇒ 56 - 4 = x + x

\n\n

⇒ 52 = 2x

\n\n

⇒ x = 26

\n\n

∴ C ਦੀ ਮੌਜੂਦਾ ਉਮਰ 26 ਸਾਲ ਹੈ।

Apply the algebraic identity a2 − b2 = (a − b)(a + b) to simplify the terms.

\n

A = (58 − 25)(58 + 25) / (46 − 37)(46 + 37) = (33 × 83) / (9 × 83) = 33 / 9 = 11 / 3.

\n

B = (26 − 15)(26 + 15) / (56 − 15)(56 + 15) = (11 × 41) / (41 × 71) = 11 / 71.

\n

Expression = 1/B − 20/A = 71 / 11 − 20 × (3 / 11) = 71/11 − 60/11 = 11/11 = 1.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

A = (582 − 252) / (462 − 372)

\n

B = (262 − 152) / (562 − 152)

\n

Formula Used:

\n

x2 − y2 = (x − y)(x + y)

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ A = ((58 − 25)(58 + 25)) / ((46 − 37)(46 + 37))

\n

⇒ A = (33 × 83) / (9 × 83)

\n

⇒ A = 33 / 9 = 11 / 3

\n

⇒ B = ((26 − 15)(26 + 15)) / ((56 − 15)(56 + 15))

\n

⇒ B = (11 × 41) / (41 × 71)

\n

⇒ B = 11 / 71

\n

⇒ Required Expression = 1/B − 20/A

\n

⇒ Required Expression = 1 / (11/71) − 20 / (11/3)

\n

⇒ Required Expression = 71 / 11 − (20 × 3) / 11

\n

⇒ Required Expression = 71 / 11 − 60 / 11

\n

⇒ Required Expression = 11 / 11 = 1

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1.

Additional Information

\n

Difference of Squares

\n

The formula a2 − b2 = (a − b)(a + b) is essential for simplifying fractions where numerator and denominator are differences of squares.

\n

Fraction Reciprocals

\n

The reciprocal of a fraction x/y is y/x. When finding 1/B, you simply flip the numerator and denominator of B.

\n

\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Value of x = 30% of 2800 = 840

\n

Value of y = 1736 ÷ (14% of 6200) = 1736 ÷ 868 = 2

\n

Required ratio x/y = 840 ÷ 2 = 420

\n

∴ The correct answer is 420.

Alternate Method

\nदिया गया है:

\n\n

पहली स्थिति के लिए मूलधन = 2,800 रुपये

\n\n

पहली स्थिति के लिए ब्याज दर = 10% प्रति वर्ष

\n\n

पहली स्थिति के लिए समय = 3 वर्ष

\n\n

दूसरे स्थिति के लिए मूलधन = 6,200 रुपये

\n\n

दूसरे स्थिति के लिए ब्याज दर = 14% प्रति वर्ष

\n\n

दूसरे स्थिति के लिए साधारण ब्याज = 1,736 रुपये

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

\\(\\rm Simple\\ Interest = \\frac{Principal \\times Rate \\times Time}{100}\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

पहली स्थिति के लिए,

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm Simple\\ Interest = \\frac{2800 \\times 10 \\times 3}{100}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm Simple\\ Interest = \\frac{84000}{100}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 840 रुपये

\n\n

इसलिए, x = 840

\n\n

दूसरी स्थिति में, हमारे पास पहले से ही साधारण ब्याज है, इसलिए हम समय (y) ज्ञात करने के लिए सूत्र का उपयोग करते हैं।

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm 1736 = \\frac{6200 \\times 14 \\times y}{100}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm 1736 = \\frac{86800y}{100}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm 1736 = 868y\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm y = \\frac{1736}{868}\\)

\n\n

⇒ y = 2

\n\n

अब, x/y का मान ज्ञात करने के लिए,

\n\n

⇒ x/y = 840/2

\n\n

⇒ x/y = 420

\n\n

∴ x/y का मान 420 है।

ପ୍ରଥମ କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ସୁଧ ହାର = 10% ବାର୍ଷିକ।

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm 1736 = \\frac{6200 \\times 14 \\times y}{100}\\)

⇒ \\(\\rm 1736 = 868y\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\rm y = \\frac{1736}{868}\\)

\n\n

⇒ y = 2

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ x/y = 840/2

\n\n

⇒ x/y = 420

\n\n

∴ x/y ର ମାନ 420।

\n90:Ta68,\n Shortcut Trick\n

In a circle, the diameter is the longest possible chord.

\n

Any other chord must be less than or equal to the diameter.

\n

Mathematically, Diameter (2r) ≥ Chord length.

\n

2r ≥ 20.5 ⇒ r ≥ 10.25.

\n

∴ The correct answer is r ≥ 10.25.", "as": "", "ks": "", "kok": "", "mni": "", "ne": "", "pa": "", "sd": "", "ur": "", "sat": "", "mai": "", "brx": "", "doi": "", "sa": "", "grt": "", "kha": "", "lus": "", "bo": "", "trp": ""}, "tags": ["Quantitative Aptitude"], "concept": "Quantitative Aptitude", "confidence": "high"}, {"qid": "6542947b272136a453a5a1dc", "qs_no": 30, "pos_marks": 3.0, "neg_marks": 1.0, "type": "mcq", "langs": {"en": {"value": "One chord of a circle is given as 20.5 cm. Then the radius (r) of the circle must be:", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "r = 10 cm"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "r &lt; 10.25"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "r &gt; 10.25"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "r &ge; 10.25"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "एक वृत्त की एक जीवा 20.5 cm दी गई है। तब वृत्त की त्रिज्या (r) होनी चाहिए:", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "r = 10 cm"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "r &lt; 10.25"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "r &gt; 10.25"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "r &ge; 10.25"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

In a circle, the diameter is the longest possible chord.

\n

Any other chord must be less than or equal to the diameter.

\n

Mathematically, Diameter (2r) ≥ Chord length.

\n

2r ≥ 20.5 ⇒ r ≥ 10.25.

\n

∴ The correct answer is r ≥ 10.25.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Length of chord = 20.5 cm

\n

Radius of the circle = r

\n

Formula/Property Used:

\n

Diameter of a circle (D) = 2 × radius (r)

\n

Geometric Property: Diameter ≥ Any Chord of the circle.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Diameter ≥ Chord

\n

⇒ 2r ≥ 20.5 cm

\n

⇒ r ≥ 20.5 ÷ 2

\n

⇒ r ≥ 10.25

\n

∴ The correct answer is r ≥ 10.25.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Longest Chord Property

\n

The diameter is the unique chord that passes through the center of the circle and is the maximum length of any chord.

\n

Chord Perpendicularity

\n

A perpendicular drawn from the center of a circle to a chord bisects the chord into two equal halves.

\n

Chord Distance from Center

\n

Longer chords are always closer to the center of the circle compared to shorter chords. The diameter (the longest chord) has a distance of zero from the center.दिया गया है:

\n\n

जीवा की लंबाई = 20.5 cm

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

वृत्त के केंद्र से जीवा पर डाला गया लंब जीवा को समद्विभाजित करता है।

\n\n

यदि एक जीवा की लंबाई c है, और केंद्र से जीवा की दूरी (लंबवत दूरी) d है, तो वृत्त की त्रिज्या (r) को समकोण त्रिभुज में पाइथागोरस प्रमेय का उपयोग करके प्राप्त किया जा सकता है:

\n\n

\\(r = \\sqrt{d^{2} + \\left(\\frac{c}{2}\\right)^{2}}\\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

आइए न्यूनतम स्थिति लेते हैं जहाँ लंबवत दूरी (d), 0 है, जो संभव नहीं है क्योंकि इसका अर्थ यह होगा कि जीवा व्यास है, इसलिए d > 0 है।

\n\n

जीवा के वृत्त के अंदर होने के लिए और व्यास के बजाय, त्रिज्या, जीवा की लंबाई के आधे से अधिक होनी चाहिए।

\n\n

r2 ≥ 20.5/2

\n\n

r ≥ 10.25

\n\n

∴ वृत्त की त्रिज्या r ≥ 10.25 होनी चाहिए।
